COURSE-BASED ASSESSMENT QUESTION BANK
Courses #27–50 | 24 Courses | 1,200 Questions
This master document contains every course supplied in the request. Each course has exactly 50 multiple-choice questions: 15 Stage 1 Basic Knowledge, 20 Stage 2 Understanding & Application, and 15 Stage 3 Deeper Application & Analysis.


Course #27 — BS Nursing
Major subject areas: Nursing process, Health assessment, Vital signs, Infection prevention, Medication safety, Fluid and electrolyte balance, Pain management, Patient safety, Medical-surgical nursing, Maternal nursing, Pediatric nursing, Mental health nursing, Community health nursing, Ethics and professional practice, Evidence-based nursing.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Nursing process? A systematic framework of assessment, diagnosis, planning, implementation, and evaluation.
A. Health assessment
B. Nursing process
C. Vital signs
D. Infection prevention
Answer: B

2. In BS Nursing, what is the primary focus of Health assessment? Systematic collection and interpretation of patient health data.
A. Health assessment
B. Vital signs
C. Infection prevention
D. Medication safety
Answer: A

3. Which purpose is most closely associated with Vital signs? Measurement and interpretation of temperature, pulse, respiration, blood pressure, and oxygenation.
A. Infection prevention
B. Medication safety
C. Fluid and electrolyte balance
D. Vital signs
Answer: D

4. Which professional activity is most directly related to Infection prevention? Practices that interrupt the transmission of microorganisms.
A. Medication safety
B. Fluid and electrolyte balance
C. Infection prevention
D. Pain management
Answer: C

5. A student is reviewing Medication safety. Which idea should be understood first? Safe preparation, administration, monitoring, and documentation of medicines.
A. Fluid and electrolyte balance
B. Medication safety
C. Pain management
D. Patient safety
Answer: B

6. Which statement best describes Fluid and electrolyte balance? Maintenance and interpretation of body fluid volume and electrolyte levels.
A. Fluid and electrolyte balance
B. Pain management
C. Patient safety
D. Medical-surgical nursing
Answer: A

7. In BS Nursing, what is the primary focus of Pain management? Assessment and multimodal management of acute and chronic pain.
A. Patient safety
B. Medical-surgical nursing
C. Maternal nursing
D. Pain management
Answer: D

8. Which purpose is most closely associated with Patient safety? Actions that reduce preventable harm during healthcare delivery.
A. Medical-surgical nursing
B. Maternal nursing
C. Patient safety
D. Pediatric nursing
Answer: C

9. Which professional activity is most directly related to Medical-surgical nursing? Nursing care for adults with acute and chronic health conditions.
A. Maternal nursing
B. Medical-surgical nursing
C. Pediatric nursing
D. Mental health nursing
Answer: B

10. A student is reviewing Maternal nursing. Which idea should be understood first? Care of women during pregnancy, labor, birth, and the postpartum period.
A. Maternal nursing
B. Pediatric nursing
C. Mental health nursing
D. Community health nursing
Answer: A

11. Which statement best describes Pediatric nursing? Health promotion and clinical care for infants, children, and adolescents.
A. Mental health nursing
B. Community health nursing
C. Ethics and professional practice
D. Pediatric nursing
Answer: D

12. In BS Nursing, what is the primary focus of Mental health nursing? Therapeutic and safety-focused care for people with mental health needs.
A. Community health nursing
B. Ethics and professional practice
C. Mental health nursing
D. Evidence-based nursing
Answer: C

13. Which purpose is most closely associated with Community health nursing? Population-focused nursing that promotes health and prevents disease.
A. Ethics and professional practice
B. Community health nursing
C. Evidence-based nursing
D. Nursing process
Answer: B

14. Which professional activity is most directly related to Ethics and professional practice? Application of ethical principles, law, accountability, and professional standards.
A. Ethics and professional practice
B. Evidence-based nursing
C. Nursing process
D. Health assessment
Answer: A

15. A student is reviewing Evidence-based nursing. Which idea should be understood first? Integration of best available evidence, clinical expertise, and patient preferences.
A. Nursing process
B. Health assessment
C. Vital signs
D. Evidence-based nursing
Answer: D

Stage 2 — Understanding & Application
16. A trainee is given a task that requires a systematic framework of assessment, diagnosis, planning, implementation, and evaluation. Which area should guide the trainee's approach?
A. Use Medication safety as the main framework for the decision
B. Apply the principles of Nursing process to the situation
C. Prioritize the considerations associated with Medical-surgical nursing
D. Base the response primarily on Community health nursing
Answer: B

17. A professional encounters a situation where the main goal is to apply the principles of Health assessment. Which choice best reflects that goal?
A. Use Fluid and electrolyte balance as the main framework for the decision
B. Prioritize the considerations associated with Maternal nursing
C. Apply the principles of Health assessment to the situation
D. Base the response primarily on Ethics and professional practice
Answer: C

18. During a routine professional situation, the team needs to use Vital signs to make a sound decision. Which action is most appropriate?
A. Use Pain management as the main framework for the decision
B. Prioritize the considerations associated with Pediatric nursing
C. Base the response primarily on Evidence-based nursing
D. Apply the principles of Vital signs to the situation
Answer: D

19. A case presents a problem closely related to Infection prevention. Which initial response best applies the concept?
A. Apply the principles of Infection prevention to the situation
B. Use Patient safety as the main framework for the decision
C. Prioritize the considerations associated with Mental health nursing
D. Base the response primarily on Nursing process
Answer: A

20. A supervisor asks a student to demonstrate practical understanding of Medication safety. Which action would provide the strongest demonstration?
A. Use Medical-surgical nursing as the main framework for the decision
B. Apply the principles of Medication safety to the situation
C. Prioritize the considerations associated with Community health nursing
D. Base the response primarily on Health assessment
Answer: B

21. A trainee is given a task that requires maintenance and interpretation of body fluid volume and electrolyte levels. Which area should guide the trainee's approach?
A. Use Maternal nursing as the main framework for the decision
B. Prioritize the considerations associated with Ethics and professional practice
C. Apply the principles of Fluid and electrolyte balance to the situation
D. Base the response primarily on Vital signs
Answer: C

D. Apply the principles of Pain management to the situation

Stage 3 — Deeper Application & Analysis
36. A complex case involves competing priorities and limited time. The evidence indicates that maintenance and interpretation of body fluid volume and electrolyte levels. Which approach best demonstrates higher-level application of Fluid and electrolyte balance?
A. Select an approach grounded primarily in Fluid and electrolyte balance and supported by the case evidence
B. Ignore Fluid and electrolyte balance and rely mainly on an unrelated routine
C. Treat Health assessment as the only relevant consideration
D. Use Patient safety without evaluating the information connected to Fluid and electrolyte balance
Answer: A

37. A professional must choose between several reasonable approaches. The central issue is assessment and multimodal management of acute and chronic pain. Which decision best reflects sound analysis of Pain management?
A. Ignore Pain management and rely mainly on an unrelated routine
B. Treat Vital signs as the only relevant consideration
C. Select an approach grounded primarily in Pain management and supported by the case evidence
D. Use Medical-surgical nursing without evaluating the information connected to Pain management
Answer: C

38. A team reviews an outcome that did not meet its intended goal. Because actions that reduce preventable harm during healthcare delivery, which interpretation of Patient safety is most defensible?
A. Select an approach grounded primarily in Patient safety and supported by the case evidence
B. Ignore Patient safety and rely mainly on an unrelated routine
C. Treat Infection prevention as the only relevant consideration
D. Use Maternal nursing without evaluating the information connected to Patient safety
Answer: A

39. A case includes incomplete information, multiple stakeholders, and potential consequences. Given that nursing care for adults with acute and chronic health conditions, which strategy best applies Medical-surgical nursing?
A. Ignore Medical-surgical nursing and rely mainly on an unrelated routine
B. Treat Medication safety as the only relevant consideration
C. Select an approach grounded primarily in Medical-surgical nursing and supported by the case evidence
D. Use Pediatric nursing without evaluating the information connected to Medical-surgical nursing
Answer: C

40. A supervisor asks a student to justify a decision in a challenging case. Considering that care of women during pregnancy, labor, birth, and the postpartum period, which response shows the strongest reasoning using Maternal nursing?
A. Select an approach grounded primarily in Maternal nursing and supported by the case evidence
B. Ignore Maternal nursing and rely mainly on an unrelated routine
C. Treat Fluid and electrolyte balance as the only relevant consideration
D. Use Mental health nursing without evaluating the information connected to Maternal nursing
Answer: A

41. A complex case involves competing priorities and limited time. The evidence indicates that health promotion and clinical care for infants, children, and adolescents. Which approach best demonstrates higher-level application of Pediatric nursing?

D. Use Nursing process without evaluating the information connected to Ethics and professional practice

Course #28 — BS Midwifery
Major subject areas: Antenatal care, Normal pregnancy, Fetal assessment, Labor assessment, Stages of labor, Intrapartum care, Postpartum care, Newborn care, Breastfeeding, Family planning, High-risk pregnancy, Obstetric emergencies, Midwifery pharmacology, Reproductive health, Professional midwifery practice.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Antenatal care? Assessment, education, screening, and monitoring during pregnancy.
A. Normal pregnancy
B. Antenatal care
C. Fetal assessment
D. Labor assessment
Answer: B

2. In BS Midwifery, what is the primary focus of Normal pregnancy? Physiologic maternal and fetal changes expected during gestation.
A. Normal pregnancy
B. Fetal assessment
C. Labor assessment
D. Stages of labor
Answer: A

3. Which purpose is most closely associated with Fetal assessment? Methods used to monitor fetal growth, wellbeing, and heart activity.
A. Labor assessment
B. Stages of labor
C. Intrapartum care
D. Fetal assessment
Answer: D

4. Which professional activity is most directly related to Labor assessment? Systematic assessment of maternal and fetal progress during labor.
A. Stages of labor
B. Intrapartum care
C. Labor assessment
D. Postpartum care
Answer: C

5. A student is reviewing Stages of labor. Which idea should be understood first? The sequential phases from cervical change through birth and placental delivery.
A. Intrapartum care
B. Stages of labor
C. Postpartum care
D. Newborn care
Answer: B

6. Which statement best describes Intrapartum care? Safe and supportive care during labor and birth.
A. Intrapartum care
B. Postpartum care
C. Newborn care
D. Breastfeeding
Answer: A

7. In BS Midwifery, what is the primary focus of Postpartum care? Assessment and support of the mother after birth.
A. Newborn care
B. Breastfeeding
C. Family planning
D. Postpartum care
Answer: D

8. Which purpose is most closely associated with Newborn care? Immediate and continuing care that supports newborn adaptation and safety.
A. Breastfeeding
B. Family planning
C. Newborn care
D. High-risk pregnancy
Answer: C

9. Which professional activity is most directly related to Breastfeeding? Support for initiation, technique, nutrition, and common breastfeeding concerns.
A. Family planning
B. Breastfeeding
C. High-risk pregnancy
D. Obstetric emergencies
Answer: B

10. A student is reviewing Family planning. Which idea should be understood first? Counseling and methods used to support informed reproductive choices.
A. Family planning
B. High-risk pregnancy
C. Obstetric emergencies
D. Midwifery pharmacology
Answer: A

11. Which statement best describes High-risk pregnancy? Recognition and management of conditions that increase maternal or fetal risk.
A. Obstetric emergencies
B. Midwifery pharmacology
C. Reproductive health
D. High-risk pregnancy
Answer: D

12. In BS Midwifery, what is the primary focus of Obstetric emergencies? Rapid recognition and response to life-threatening maternal or fetal complications.
A. Midwifery pharmacology
B. Reproductive health
C. Obstetric emergencies
D. Professional midwifery practice
Answer: C

13. Which purpose is most closely associated with Midwifery pharmacology? Safe use of medicines relevant to pregnancy, birth, postpartum, and newborn care.
A. Reproductive health
B. Midwifery pharmacology
C. Professional midwifery practice
D. Antenatal care
Answer: B

14. Which professional activity is most directly related to Reproductive health? Promotion of healthy reproductive function and informed reproductive decisions.
A. Reproductive health
B. Professional midwifery practice
C. Antenatal care
D. Normal pregnancy
Answer: A

15. A student is reviewing Professional midwifery practice. Which idea should be understood first? Ethical, evidence-informed, culturally responsive, and accountable midwifery care.
A. Antenatal care
B. Normal pregnancy
C. Fetal assessment
D. Professional midwifery practice
Answer: D

Course #29 — BS Pharmacy
Major subject areas: Pharmaceutical calculations, Pharmaceutics, Pharmacokinetics, Pharmacodynamics, Medicinal chemistry, Pharmacology, Drug interactions, Adverse drug reactions, Medication counseling, Prescription and dispensing, Sterile products, Hospital pharmacy, Community pharmacy, Pharmacovigilance, Professional pharmacy practice.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Pharmaceutical calculations? Mathematical methods used to determine doses, concentrations, quantities, and rates.
A. Pharmaceutics
B. Pharmaceutical calculations
C. Pharmacokinetics
D. Pharmacodynamics
Answer: B

2. In BS Pharmacy, what is the primary focus of Pharmaceutics? Science of designing and preparing dosage forms.
A. Pharmaceutics
B. Pharmacokinetics
C. Pharmacodynamics
D. Medicinal chemistry
Answer: A

3. Which purpose is most closely associated with Pharmacokinetics? What the body does to a drug through absorption, distribution, metabolism, and excretion.
A. Pharmacodynamics
B. Medicinal chemistry
C. Pharmacology
D. Pharmacokinetics
Answer: D

A. Medicinal chemistry
B. Pharmacology
C. Pharmacodynamics
D. Drug interactions
Answer: C

A. Pharmacology
B. Medicinal chemistry
C. Drug interactions
D. Adverse drug reactions
Answer: B

A. Pharmacology
B. Drug interactions
C. Adverse drug reactions
D. Medication counseling
Answer: A

D. Drug interactions

Course #31 — BS Medical Technology
Major subject areas: Clinical chemistry, Hematology, Clinical microscopy, Microbiology, Parasitology, Immunology, Blood banking, Histopathology, Specimen collection, Quality control, Biosafety, Laboratory instrumentation, Molecular diagnostics, Laboratory information systems, Professional laboratory practice.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Clinical chemistry? Analysis of chemical constituents in body fluids for diagnosis and monitoring.
A. Hematology
B. Clinical chemistry
C. Clinical microscopy
D. Microbiology
Answer: B

A. Hematology
B. Clinical microscopy
C. Microbiology
D. Parasitology
Answer: A

A. Microbiology
B. Parasitology
C. Immunology
D. Clinical microscopy

B. Immunology

D. Blood banking

B. Parasitology
C. Blood banking
D. Histopathology

D. Specimen collection

Course #32 — BS Radiologic Technology
Major subject areas: Radiographic positioning, Radiation physics, Radiation protection, Radiographic exposure factors, Image quality, Patient care, Contrast media, Computed tomography, Magnetic resonance imaging, Ultrasound, Mammography, Fluoroscopy, Trauma radiography, Quality assurance, Professional radiologic practice.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Radiographic positioning? Proper patient positioning to obtain diagnostic images.

B. Radiographic positioning

D. Radiographic exposure factors

B. Radiation protection

D. Image quality

Major subject areas: Macronutrients, Micronutrients, Digestion and absorption, Energy metabolism, Nutritional assessment, Diet planning, Medical nutrition therapy, Food service management, Food safety, Community nutrition, Maternal and child nutrition, Sports nutrition, Public health nutrition, Nutrition counseling, Nutrition research and ethics.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Macronutrients? Carbohydrates, proteins, and fats that supply energy and structural functions.

B. Macronutrients
C. Digestion and absorption
D. Energy metabolism

D. Nutritional assessment

Course #34 — BS Psychology
Major subject areas: General psychology, Developmental psychology, Cognitive psychology, Social psychology, Personality, Abnormal psychology, Psychological assessment, Research methods, Statistics, Learning, Emotion and motivation, Biological psychology, Counseling foundations, Industrial-organizational psychology, Ethics in psychology.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes General psychology? Foundational study of behavior and mental processes.
A. Developmental psychology
B. General psychology
C. Cognitive psychology
D. Social psychology

B. Cognitive psychology

D. Personality

Course #35 — BS Hospitality Management
Major subject areas: Hospitality operations, Front office, Housekeeping, Food and beverage service, Menu planning, Kitchen operations, Hospitality marketing, Revenue management, Customer experience, Event management, Hospitality human resources, Cost control, Food safety and sanitation, Sustainable hospitality, Service recovery.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Hospitality operations? Coordination of lodging, food, beverage, and guest services.
A. Front office
B. Hospitality operations
C. Housekeeping
D. Food and beverage service
Answer: B

B. Housekeeping
C. Food and beverage service
D. Menu planning
Answer: A

C. Kitchen operations
D. Housekeeping

B. Kitchen operations
C. Food and beverage service
D. Hospitality marketing

B. Menu planning
C. Hospitality marketing
D. Revenue management
Answer: B

B. Hospitality marketing

Course #37 — BS Hotel and Restaurant Management
Major subject areas: Hotel operations, Front desk operations, Room division, Housekeeping management, Restaurant operations, Menu management, Food production, Purchasing and inventory, Cost and yield control, Service quality, Human resource management, Hospitality accounting, Food safety and sanitation, Events and banquets, Guest relations and service recovery.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Hotel operations? Integrated management of lodging departments and guest services.
A. Front desk operations
B. Hotel operations
C. Room division
D. Housekeeping management
Answer: B

2. In BS Hotel and Restaurant Management, what is the primary focus of Front desk operations? Reservation, registration, room assignment, billing, and guest assistance.
A. Front desk operations
B. Room division
C. Housekeeping management
D. Restaurant operations
Answer: A

3. Which purpose is most closely associated with Room division? Coordination of front office and housekeeping functions.
A. Housekeeping management
B. Restaurant operations
C. Menu management
D. Room division
Answer: D

4. Which professional activity is most directly related to Housekeeping management? Planning and supervision of room cleanliness and accommodation standards.
A. Restaurant operations
B. Menu management
C. Housekeeping management
D. Food production
Answer: C

5. A student is reviewing Restaurant operations. Which idea should be understood first? Management of dining service, staffing, production, and guest experience.
A. Menu management
B. Restaurant operations
C. Food production
D. Purchasing and inventory
Answer: B

6. Which statement best describes Menu management? Planning and evaluation of restaurant offerings.
A. Menu management
B. Food production
C. Purchasing and inventory
D. Cost and yield control
Answer: A

7. In BS Hotel and Restaurant Management, what is the primary focus of Food production? Organized preparation and presentation of food.
A. Purchasing and inventory
B. Cost and yield control
C. Service quality
D. Food production
Answer: D

8. Which purpose is most closely associated with Purchasing and inventory? Procurement, receiving, storage, and control of supplies.
A. Cost and yield control
B. Service quality
C. Purchasing and inventory
D. Human resource management
Answer: C

9. Which professional activity is most directly related to Cost and yield control? Measurement of food, labor, and operational costs.
A. Service quality
B. Cost and yield control
C. Human resource management
D. Hospitality accounting
Answer: B

10. A student is reviewing Service quality. Which idea should be understood first? Standards and systems used to maintain consistent guest service.
A. Service quality
B. Human resource management
C. Hospitality accounting
D. Food safety and sanitation
Answer: A

11. Which statement best describes Human resource management? Staffing, training, scheduling, and performance management.
A. Hospitality accounting
B. Food safety and sanitation
C. Events and banquets
D. Human resource management
Answer: D

12. In BS Hotel and Restaurant Management, what is the primary focus of Hospitality accounting? Financial recording and analysis for hotel and restaurant operations.
A. Food safety and sanitation
B. Events and banquets
C. Hospitality accounting
D. Guest relations and service recovery
Answer: C

13. Which purpose is most closely associated with Food safety and sanitation? Controls that prevent contamination and foodborne illness.
A. Events and banquets
B. Food safety and sanitation
C. Guest relations and service recovery
D. Hotel operations
Answer: B

A. Events and banquets
B. Guest relations and service recovery
C. Hotel operations
D. Front desk operations
Answer: A

C. Room division
D. Guest relations and service recovery

Course #39 — BS Marine Engineering
Major subject areas: Marine diesel engines, Engine-room systems, Fuel and lubrication systems, Cooling systems, Pumps and compressors, Boilers and steam systems, Electrical systems, Automation and control, Maintenance, Troubleshooting, Engine-room safety, Pollution prevention, Watchkeeping, Emergency machinery operations, Marine engineering regulations.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Marine diesel engines? Operation and principles of large internal-combustion engines used at sea.
A. Engine-room systems

D. Cooling systems

Course #42 — AB Political Science
Major subject areas: Political theory, Philippine government, Comparative politics, International relations, Public policy, Political economy, Constitutional law, Public administration, Political parties, Elections and voting, Political behavior, Governance, Human rights, Research methods, Political communication.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge

B. Political theory
C. Comparative politics
D. International relations
Answer: B

Course #44 — BS Social Work
Major subject areas: Social work values and ethics, Human behavior and social environment, Social casework, Group work, Community organization, Social welfare policy, Social work assessment, Intervention planning, Crisis intervention, Child and family welfare, Mental health social work, Medical social work, Research in social work, Advocacy, Social work documentation.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Social work values and ethics? Principles guiding responsible, respectful, and accountable social work practice.
A. Human behavior and social environment

C. Social casework
D. Group work

C. Group work
D. Community organization

D. Social casework

C. Group work
D. Social work assessment

C. Social work assessment
D. Intervention planning
Answer: B

Course #45 — AB Journalism
Major subject areas: News values, News gathering, Interviewing, News writing, Feature writing, Investigative journalism, Editing, Source verification, Media law, Journalistic ethics, Photojournalism, Broadcast journalism, Digital journalism, Data journalism, Press freedom and responsibility.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes News values? Criteria used to judge the significance and newsworthiness of events.
A. News gathering
B. News values
C. Interviewing
D. News writing

B. Interviewing
C. News writing
D. Feature writing

B. Feature writing
C. Investigative journalism
D. Interviewing

Course #46 — BS Public Administration
Major subject areas: Public administration theory, Philippine bureaucracy, Public policy, Public budgeting, Human resource management, Public financial management, Local governance, Public service delivery, Administrative law, Organizational behavior, Program evaluation, Good governance, E-government, Disaster governance, Public ethics.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Public administration theory? Concepts explaining how government organizations and public services are managed.
A. Philippine bureaucracy
B. Public administration theory
C. Public policy
D. Public budgeting
Answer: B

2. In BS Public Administration, what is the primary focus of Philippine bureaucracy? Structure and functioning of government administrative institutions in the philippines.
A. Philippine bureaucracy
B. Public policy
C. Public budgeting
D. Human resource management
Answer: A

3. Which purpose is most closely associated with Public policy? Process of designing, implementing, and evaluating government responses.
A. Public budgeting
B. Human resource management
C. Public financial management
D. Public policy
Answer: D

4. Which professional activity is most directly related to Public budgeting? Planning and allocation of public resources through government budgets.
A. Human resource management
B. Public financial management
C. Public budgeting
D. Local governance
Answer: C

5. A student is reviewing Human resource management. Which idea should be understood first? Recruitment, development, performance, and discipline of public employees.
A. Public financial management
B. Human resource management
C. Local governance
D. Public service delivery
Answer: B

6. Which statement best describes Public financial management? Systems for managing government revenues, expenditures, and accountability.
A. Public financial management
B. Local governance
C. Public service delivery
D. Administrative law
Answer: A

7. In BS Public Administration, what is the primary focus of Local governance? Administration and service delivery at local government levels.
A. Public service delivery
B. Administrative law
C. Organizational behavior
D. Local governance
Answer: D

8. Which purpose is most closely associated with Public service delivery? Process of providing government programs and services to citizens.
A. Administrative law
B. Organizational behavior
C. Public service delivery
D. Program evaluation
Answer: C

9. Which professional activity is most directly related to Administrative law? Legal rules governing public agencies and administrative action.
A. Organizational behavior
B. Administrative law
C. Program evaluation
D. Good governance
Answer: B

10. A student is reviewing Organizational behavior. Which idea should be understood first? Behavior of individuals and groups within public organizations.
A. Organizational behavior
B. Program evaluation
C. Good governance
D. E-government
Answer: A

A. Good governance
B. E-government
C. Disaster governance
D. Program evaluation
Answer: D

12. In BS Public Administration, what is the primary focus of Good governance? Principles including accountability, transparency, participation, and responsiveness.
A. E-government
B. Disaster governance
C. Good governance
D. Public ethics
Answer: C

13. Which purpose is most closely associated with E-government? Use of digital technologies to improve government services and administration.
A. Disaster governance
B. E-government
C. Public ethics
D. Public administration theory
Answer: B

14. Which professional activity is most directly related to Disaster governance? Public-sector coordination and management of disaster risks and responses.
A. Disaster governance
B. Public ethics
C. Public administration theory
D. Philippine bureaucracy
Answer: A

B. Philippine bureaucracy
C. Public policy
D. Public ethics
Answer: D

1. Which statement best describes Graphic design? Visual communication using composition, typography, imagery, and design principles.

B. Graphic design

D. Digital illustration

D. Photography

Course #48 — BS Biology
Major subject areas: Cell biology, Genetics, Molecular biology, Evolution, Ecology, Microbiology, Botany, Zoology, Physiology, Biochemistry, Developmental biology, Taxonomy, Biostatistics, Laboratory methods, Conservation biology.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Cell biology? Study of cell structure, function, and processes.
A. Genetics
B. Cell biology
C. Molecular biology
D. Evolution
Answer: B

2. In BS Biology, what is the primary focus of Genetics? Study of heredity, genes, and variation.
A. Genetics
B. Molecular biology
C. Evolution
D. Ecology
Answer: A

3. Which purpose is most closely associated with Molecular biology? Study of biological processes at the molecular level.
A. Evolution
B. Ecology
C. Microbiology
D. Molecular biology
Answer: D

4. Which professional activity is most directly related to Evolution? Study of changes in populations and the processes producing biological diversity.
A. Ecology
B. Microbiology
C. Evolution
D. Botany
Answer: C

A. Microbiology
B. Ecology
C. Botany
D. Zoology
Answer: B

6. Which statement best describes Microbiology? Study of microorganisms and their roles in health and ecosystems.
A. Microbiology
B. Botany
C. Zoology
D. Physiology
Answer: A

7. In BS Biology, what is the primary focus of Botany? Study of plants and their structure, function, and diversity.
A. Zoology
B. Physiology
C. Biochemistry
D. Botany
Answer: D

A. Physiology
B. Biochemistry
C. Zoology
D. Developmental biology
Answer: C

A. Biochemistry
B. Physiology
C. Developmental biology
D. Taxonomy
Answer: B

B. Developmental biology
C. Taxonomy
D. Biostatistics

B. Agronomy
C. Horticulture

Course #50 — BS Fisheries
Major subject areas: Fish biology, Aquaculture, Fisheries ecology, Fish nutrition, Fish health, Fisheries management, Capture fisheries, Fishing gear technology, Postharvest fisheries, Limnology, Oceanography, Aquatic pollution, Fisheries economics, Fisheries law and policy, Fisheries research methods.
Stage 1 — Basic Knowledge
1. Which statement best describes Fish biology? Structure, function, growth, and life processes of fish.
A. Aquaculture
B. Fish biology
C. Fisheries ecology
D. Fish nutrition
Answer: B

2. In BS Fisheries, what is the primary focus of Aquaculture? Controlled production of aquatic organisms.
A. Aquaculture
B. Fisheries ecology
C. Fish nutrition
D. Fish health
Answer: A

3. Which purpose is most closely associated with Fisheries ecology? Relationships among aquatic organisms, habitats, and environmental conditions.
A. Fish nutrition
B. Fish health
C. Fisheries management
D. Fisheries ecology
Answer: D

4. Which professional activity is most directly related to Fish nutrition? Nutrient requirements and feeding of cultured and wild fish.
A. Fish health
B. Fisheries management
C. Fish nutrition
D. Capture fisheries
Answer: C

5. A student is reviewing Fish health. Which idea should be understood first? Prevention, diagnosis, and management of disease in aquatic organisms.
A. Fisheries management
B. Fish health
C. Capture fisheries
D. Fishing gear technology
Answer: B

6. Which statement best describes Fisheries management? Regulation and stewardship of fish populations and fisheries.
A. Fisheries management
B. Capture fisheries
C. Fishing gear technology
D. Postharvest fisheries
Answer: A

7. In BS Fisheries, what is the primary focus of Capture fisheries? Harvesting of fish and other aquatic resources from natural waters.
A. Fishing gear technology
B. Postharvest fisheries
C. Limnology
D. Capture fisheries
Answer: D

8. Which purpose is most closely associated with Fishing gear technology? Design and use of fishing gear for effective and responsible harvest.
A. Postharvest fisheries
B. Limnology
C. Fishing gear technology
D. Oceanography
Answer: C

9. Which professional activity is most directly related to Postharvest fisheries? Handling, preservation, processing, and quality control of aquatic products.
A. Limnology
B. Postharvest fisheries
C. Oceanography
D. Aquatic pollution
Answer: B

10. A student is reviewing Limnology. Which idea should be understood first? Study of inland waters and their biological and physical processes.
A. Limnology
B. Oceanography
C. Aquatic pollution
D. Fisheries economics
Answer: A

11. Which statement best describes Oceanography? Study of marine environments and processes.
A. Aquatic pollution
B. Fisheries economics
C. Fisheries law and policy
D. Oceanography
Answer: D

12. In BS Fisheries, what is the primary focus of Aquatic pollution? Effects and management of contaminants in aquatic ecosystems.
A. Fisheries economics
B. Fisheries law and policy
C. Aquatic pollution
D. Fisheries research methods
Answer: C

13. Which purpose is most closely associated with Fisheries economics? Economic analysis of fisheries, markets, and resource use.
A. Fisheries law and policy
B. Fisheries economics
C. Fisheries research methods
D. Fish biology
Answer: B

14. Which professional activity is most directly related to Fisheries law and policy? Rules and governance systems for aquatic resources.
A. Fisheries law and policy
B. Fisheries research methods
C. Fish biology
D. Aquaculture
Answer: A

D. Fisheries research methods
